## Seeded round 5 2026-11-04 (Mark-authorized)
- [ ] [P1] [decomposed] MissionEditor load then save silently drops patrol routes, defend_until and directive — `scripts/tools/mission_editor.gd` `_on_load_pressed()` (line ~396) copies grid, objective, terrain, cells and spawns into editor fields but never reads `mission.enemy_patrol_routes`, `mission.defend_until` or `mission.directive`, and `_on_save_pressed()` (line ~466) builds a fresh `MissionData.new()` that never sets them, so opening any existing mission and pressing Save permanently erases data (repo scan: 13 of the missions/*.tres files carry `enemy_patrol_routes` with Vector2i waypoints and mission_57.tres carries `defend_until`; the existing 9 tests in tests/test_mission_editor.gd only round-trip walls, spawns and objective fields, never these). Fix: add editor fields `_defend_until: int = -1`, `_directive: int = 0` and `_patrol_by_cell: Dictionary = {}` (enemy spawn cell to route Array); `_on_load_pressed` fills them (`_patrol_by_cell[mission.enemy_spawns[i]] = mission.patrol_route_for_index(i)` for non-empty routes); `_on_save_pressed` writes `mission.defend_until`, `mission.directive` and builds `mission.enemy_patrol_routes` in `_enemy_spawn_order` order using `_patrol_by_cell.get(cell, [])` (an erased or moved spawn naturally loses its route); `_on_new_pressed` resets all three. Do not touch battle.gd or mission_select.gd. Tests in tests/test_mission_editor.gd (use the existing `_save_and_reload()` helper): load mission_57 through `editor._load_option` selection then save and assert `defend_until` equals the original; load a mission whose `enemy_patrol_routes` is non-empty (find one by scanning missions/mission_*.tres at runtime) then save and assert the routes are equal; erasing that enemy spawn drops only its route; `_on_new_pressed` then save gives `defend_until == -1` and empty routes. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_mission_editor.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw Passing/Tests totals show 0 failing (GUT exits 0 even on failures; the new tests fail or error before the change).. {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] MissionEditor dropdowns cannot represent Defend objective or the 9 non-wasteland/city themes — `scripts/tools/mission_editor.gd` `_OBJECTIVE_NAMES` (line 46) has 5 entries (Eliminate to Assassinate) but `MissionData.objective_type` documents 6 (5 = DEFEND, used by missions/mission_57.tres), so `_objective_option.select(_objective_type)` in `_on_load_pressed` selects nothing for a Defend mission and the author cannot choose Defend at all; the theme dropdown (line ~323) only offers "wasteland" and "city" and `_on_load_pressed` does `_theme_option.select(0 if _terrain_theme == "wasteland" else 1)`, so loading a dock/farmland/frozen/industrial/military/prison/suburban/subway/toxic mission (all exist in missions/*.tres) displays "city" and re-selecting anything overwrites the real theme. Fix: append "Defend" to `_OBJECTIVE_NAMES`; add `const _THEMES := ["wasteland", "city", "industrial", "dock", "farmland", "frozen", "military", "prison", "suburban", "subway", "toxic"]`, populate `_theme_option` from it and select with `_THEMES.find(_terrain_theme)` (fall back to adding the unknown string as an extra item so it is never relabeled). Tests in tests/test_mission_editor.gd: `MissionEditor._OBJECTIVE_NAMES.size() == 6`; loading mission_57 leaves `editor._objective_option.selected == 5`; a runtime scan of every missions/mission_*.tres asserts each `terrain_theme` is in `MissionEditor._THEMES` (list offenders by file name); loading a frozen-theme mission shows the "frozen" item as selected. - [ ] [P3] [decomposed] SaveManager.save_save mutates the caller's Dictionary (adds save_version) — `scripts/save/save_manager.gd` `save_save()` does `data["save_version"] = SAVE_VERSION` on the dictionary it was handed (GDScript Dictionaries are references), which already forced `InputRemapper.save_bindings()` to pass `current_bindings.duplicate()` and is a latent pollution bug for every other caller (a caller that keeps using its dict, e.g. a settings or roster dict, now has a foreign key in it). Fix: at the top of `save_save` after the name check, `data = data.duplicate()` (shallow is enough for the top-level stamp) and keep the existing `if not data.has("save_version")` stamping, so the written file is unchanged while the caller's dict is not. - [ ] [P3] [decomposed] CodexManager accepts arbitrary category strings and persists phantom categories — `scripts/codex/codex_manager.gd` `mark_seen(category, key)` only rejects an empty key, so a typo'd category (the file's own comment says the CAT_* constants exist to stop exactly this) silently creates a new bucket, saves it, emits `entry_revealed`, and `_load()` re-imports any category found in a hand-edited save; `seen_count`/`has_seen` for the real categories never see it. Fix: add `const VALID_CATEGORIES: Array[String] = [CAT_ENEMY, CAT_SOLDIER, CAT_TECH, CAT_FACTION]`; `mark_seen` returns false (no save, no signal) when `not VALID_CATEGORIES.has(category)`; `_load()` skips categories not in the list. Tests in tests/test_codex_manager.gd and tests/test_codex_manager_load.gd: `mark_seen("enemy", "grunt")` (typo) is false, `seen_count("enemy")` is 0 and no `entry_revealed` signal fires (use `watch_signals`); a codex save containing `{"seen": {"bogus": ["x"], "enemies": ["grunt"]}}` loads only `enemies` (`has_seen(CodexManager.CAT_ENEMY, "grunt")` true, `seen_count("bogus") == 0`); existing tests stay green. ## Seeded round 6 2026-11-06 (Mark-authorized)
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] `RosterManager.normalize_loaded_entries` duplicate-rename can itself create a duplicate name — `scripts/roster/roster_manager.gd` `normalize_loaded_entries` (line ~573) keeps a per-base-name counter and renames the Nth repeat to `"%s-%d" % [name, count]` without checking whether that name already exists elsewhere in the list, so loaded names `["A", "A", "A-2"]` become `["A", "A-2", "A-2"]` (a python port of the function reproduces it); `_find_entry`, `set_deployed`, `sync_from_battle` (keyed by `u.name`) and `recruit_captured` all key on the name, so the two "A-2" soldiers alias each other (one is silently updated/dropped when the other deploys or dies). Existing tests (tests/test_roster_normalize.gd, tests/test_roster_load_hardening.gd) only cover `["Vega","Vega","Vega"]`. Fix: first pass builds a `used` Dictionary of every sanitized name in the list; second pass, for each repeat of an already-seen name, loop `n` upward from the counter until `"%s-%d" % [name, n]` is not in `used`, assign it and add it to `used`. Add tests/test_roster_normalize_collision.gd (`extends GutTest`): `[{name:"A"},{name:"A"},{name:"A-2"}]` normalizes to exactly `["A","A-3","A-2"]`; the result names always form a set of the same size as the list (check with a Dictionary) for `["A","A","A","A-2","A-3"]`; the original `["Vega","Vega","Vega"]` still gives `Vega, Vega-2, Vega-3`; and `deployed` capping is unchanged. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_roster_normalize_collision.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the A/A/A-2 case fails before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] Benched survivors are never re-deployed, so a won mission can leave ZERO deployed soldiers and the next mission spawns no squad — `RosterManager.sync_from_battle` (scripts/roster/roster_manager.gd) drops only the soldiers in `fallen_names` and keeps benched ones with `deployed=false`; if all (or the only) deployed soldiers fall in a victory while others are benched, `get_deployed_count() == 0`, and `battle.gd:_spawn_units` (line ~612-622) loops over `get_deployed_entries()` so zero player units spawn; the roster screen's Bench button can also leave 0 deployed with no warning (`scripts/mission/roster_view.gd` only warns about a missing Medic). Fix (no battle.gd edit): at the end of `sync_from_battle` call a new `_ensure_squad_deployed()` that, when `_entries` is non-empty and `get_deployed_count() == 0`, marks the first `MAX_SQUAD` entries deployed (then `_save_roster()` once); and in roster_view.gd add `static func empty_squad_warning(deployed: int, total: int) -> String` returning "No soldiers deployed - the next mission would start with no squad" only when `total > 0 and deployed == 0` (else ""), shown as an orange Label in `_refresh()` next to the no-medic warning. Tests in new tests/test_roster_empty_squad.gd: `RosterManager.reset_roster()` (2 default soldiers), `set_deployed` both to false, then `var none: Array[Unit] = []; RosterManager.sync_from_battle(none, [])` gives `get_deployed_count() == 2`; with 6 benched-or-deployed soldiers and all 4 deployed names in `fallen_names` the 2 survivors get deployed; a squad that already has 1 deployed soldier is left untouched (count stays 1); an empty roster stays empty without error; `RosterView.empty_squad_warning(0, 3) != ""`, `(2, 3) == ""`, `(0, 0) == ""`; call `RosterManager.reset_roster()` at the end of each test to restore state. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_roster_empty_squad.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the deployed==2 case fails before). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] Destructive one-click buttons have no confirmation: "Reset Techs" and slot "Delete" — `scripts/mission/tech_tree.gd` `_on_reset_pressed()` calls `TechManager.reset_techs()` immediately (all research gone, credits/materials already spent are not refunded) and `scripts/mission/save_slot_select.gd` `_on_slot_deleted()` deletes the slot's roster/tech/settings/battle/codex saves on a single press, with the Delete button sitting next to Continue; grep of scripts/ for "confirm" finds no confirmation anywhere. ## Seeded round 8 2026-11-09 (Mark-authorized)
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] BuildInfo.is_valid_version accepts a trailing newline, so a version of "1.2.3\n" is reported as valid - `scripts/release/build_info.gd` `is_valid_version` compiles `^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$` and returns `regex.search(v) != null`; PCRE `$` also matches just before a final newline, so `"1.2.3\n"` passes (confirmed by running it: `assert_false(BuildInfo.is_valid_version("1.2.3\n"))` fails) and would be written into build metadata, whereas `ReleaseCheck._is_valid_version` and `ItchReadinessCheck.validate_itch_channel_name` already guard this with `result.get_string() == v`. Fix: `var result: RegExMatch = regex.search(v)` then `return result != null and result.get_string() == v`. Extend tests/test_build_info.gd with `is_valid_version("1.2.3\n")` false, `"1.2.3"` true, `"10.20.30"` true, `"1.2"` false, `"1.2.3.4"` false, `"v1.2.3"` false, `""` false, `" 1.2.3"` false. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_build_info.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the trailing-newline case fails before). {cat: infra; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] Objective.progress_ratio for ELIMINATE is not clamped, so a bad context gives a negative or over-1.0 progress ratio - `scripts/mission/objective.gd` `progress_ratio` case 0 returns `float(enemies_total - enemies_alive) / float(enemies_total)` with no clamp (the SURVIVE and DEFEND cases already use `clampf`); with `enemies_total=2, enemies_alive=3` (reinforcements spawned after the total was captured) it returns -0.5 and with `enemies_alive=-1` it returns 1.5 (both confirmed by running), which any progress bar or `MissionHub` percent format would show as a negative or >100 percent. Fix: wrap in `clampf(..., 0.0, 1.0)`. Extend tests/test_objective_progress.gd (it uses `Objective.progress_ratio(0, {...})`): `{enemies_total: 2, enemies_alive: 3}` is 0.0, `{enemies_total: 2, enemies_alive: -1}` is 1.0, `{enemies_total: 4, enemies_alive: 1}` is 0.75 (assert_almost_eq 0.001), `{enemies_total: 0, enemies_alive: 5}` stays 1.0. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_objective_progress.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the alive>total and alive<0 cases fail before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] WeightedPick lets a negative weight corrupt the buckets so later entries can never be picked - `scripts/battle/weighted_pick.gd` `cumulative` and `index_for_roll` sum weights as-is; for weights `[5, -3, 2]` the running totals are 5, 2, 4 so every roll lands in index 0 (index 2 is unreachable; confirmed by running 4 rolls, all returned 0), and tests/test_weighted_pick.gd only covers zero weights. Fix: treat any weight below 0 as 0 in both functions (`var wt: int = maxi(int(w), 0)` inside each loop). Extend tests/test_weighted_pick.gd (it preloads the script as `WeightedPick`): `cumulative([5, -3, 2]) == [5, 5, 7]`; `index_for_roll([5, -3, 2], r)` is 0 for r in 0..4 and 2 for r in 5 and 6, never 1; `index_for_roll([-1, -2], 0) == -1`; `index_for_roll([3, 0, 1], 3) == 2` still holds. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_weighted_pick.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the negative-weight cases fail before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] TurnSnapshot.capture crashes on a non-Dictionary entry instead of skipping it - `scripts/turn/turn_snapshot.gd` `capture` does `(s as Dictionary).duplicate(true)` for every element; `capture([{"a": 1}, 5])` raises a runtime error on the int and returns an incomplete array (confirmed by running: size was not 1 after the error), and `restore` delegates to it, so one corrupt saved entry breaks an undo/restore. Keep the file's 4-space indentation. Fix: inside the loop `if typeof(s) != TYPE_DICTIONARY: continue` before the duplicate. Extend tests/test_turn_snapshot_edges.gd (tab-indented): `capture([{"a": 1}, 5, null, "x", {"b": 2}])` has size 2, `snap[0]["a"] == 1`, `snap[1]["b"] == 2`; `restore` of that result has the same size; and the result entries are deep copies (mutating the source `{"b": 2}` afterwards does not change `snap[1]`). VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_turn_snapshot_edges.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the mixed-entry case fails before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] ItchUploader.build_butler_command builds a butler push argv with an empty build path - `scripts/release/itch_uploader.gd` `build_butler_command(build_path, channel)` validates only the channel; `build_butler_command("", "windows")` returns a 4-element argv with an empty third argument (confirmed by running: size 4, expected 0), so `butler push "" game:windows` would run against the working directory or fail obscurely. Fix: after the channel check add `if build_path.strip_edges().is_empty(): push_error("Empty itch build path"); return PackedStringArray()`. Extend tests/test_itch_uploader.gd (push_error on stderr is expected there, see the invalid-channel test): `build_butler_command("", "windows").size() == 0`, `("   ", "windows").size() == 0`, and `("build/xlite.zip", "windows").size() == 4` still. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_itch_uploader.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the empty-path cases fail before). {cat: infra; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] StatusModifiers.get_move_speed_multiplier is order-dependent and ignores slowed, disagreeing with move_multiplier_percent - `scripts/units/status_modifiers.gd` `get_move_speed_multiplier(effects)` returns 0.5 as soon as it sees "blind" and only returns 0.0 if "stunned" comes earlier, so `["blind", "stunned"]` gives 0.5 (confirmed by running; a stunned unit must be 0.0), and it never handles "slowed" (`["slowed"]` gives 1.0 while `move_multiplier_percent(["slowed"])` is 50); the function has no test (tests/test_status_modifiers.gd only calls `move_multiplier_percent` and `aim_penalty`). Fix: make it `return float(move_multiplier_percent(effects)) / 100.0` so there is one source of truth. Extend tests/test_status_modifiers.gd with `assert_almost_eq(StatusModifiers.get_move_speed_multiplier(X), Y, 0.001)` for `["blind", "stunned"]` -> 0.0, `["stunned", "blind"]` -> 0.0, `["slowed"]` -> 0.5, `["blind"]` -> 0.5, `["slowed", "blind"]` -> 0.5, `[]` -> 1.0, `["suppressed"]` -> 1.0. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_status_modifiers.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the blind+stunned and slowed cases fail before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [x] [P3] [decomposed] SpiralCells.count_in_radius(-1) returns 1 while filled_area(center, -1) is empty - `scripts/grid/spiral_cells.gd` `count_in_radius` computes `side = 2 * radius + 1` and returns `side * side`, so radius -1 gives 1 (confirmed by running; expected 0) and radius -2 gives 9, but `filled_area` for any negative radius returns an empty array (pinned by tests/test_spiral_cells_filled_area.gd `test_negative_radius_is_empty`), so the count and the area disagree. ## Seeded round 9 2026-11-10 (Mark-authorized, addendum)
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] CameraInput.remap_touch_to_camera passes NaN straight through, so a degenerate pinch (two touches at the same point gives 0/0) sets the camera zoom to NaN and the pan to NaN - `scripts/battle/camera_input.gd` `remap_touch_to_camera(delta, pinch_scale)` clamps with `clampf`, which does not clamp NaN (confirmed by running it: `remap_touch_to_camera(Vector2(3, 4), NAN)` returns zoom nan, and `Vector2(NAN, 4)` returns pan (nan, 4)); infinity is already clamped to the bounds. Fix with explicit types and no Variant inference: `var dx: float = 0.0 if is_nan(delta.x) else delta.x`, `var dy: float = 0.0 if is_nan(delta.y) else delta.y`, `var scale: float = 1.0 if is_nan(pinch_scale) else pinch_scale`, then clamp `dx`, `dy` and `scale * PINCH_ZOOM_SENSITIVITY` exactly as before (a draft of this change passes the 5 existing tests and the new one). Extend tests/test_camera_input.gd with `test_nan_inputs_are_neutral`: `var r: Dictionary = CameraInput.remap_touch_to_camera(Vector2(NAN, 4.0), NAN)`, `assert_eq(r["zoom"], 1.0)`, `assert_eq(r["pan"], Vector2(0.0, 4.0))`; keep the five existing tests. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_camera_input.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the NaN test fails before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}

### FEATURE: Localization hooks - acceptance: screen text that goes through `Loc.t` resolves from the TranslationServer and falls back to the shipped English when no translation is loaded, the hook is wired into the save slot picker and the controls help, a CSV extraction tool lists every key, and a test keeps one key from carrying two different English texts; no translation files ship in this feature and the English text does not change.
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] Add the Loc hook and use it in the save slot picker - create `scripts/ui/loc.gd` with `class_name Loc`, `extends RefCounted` and two static functions: `t(key: String, fallback: String) -> String` (returns `fallback` for an empty key; otherwise `var translated: String = String(TranslationServer.translate(StringName(key)))` and returns `fallback` when `translated` is empty or equal to `key`, else `translated`) and `tf(key: String, fallback: String, args: Array) -> String` (calls `t`, then for each index `i` replaces `"{%d}" % i` with `str(args[i])`; no `%` formatting so a translation without placeholders cannot raise). In `scripts/mission/save_slot_select.gd` route the fixed texts through it with the SAME English as today: `Loc.t("ui.save_slots.title", "Save Slots")`, `Loc.t("ui.save_slots.hint", "Continue a saved run, or start a new one.")`, `Loc.t("ui.common.back", "Back")` for the Back button, `Loc.t("ui.save_slots.continue", "▶  Continue")`, `Loc.t("ui.save_slots.delete", "Delete")` and `Loc.t("ui.save_slots.start_new", "+  Start New Run")`. Create `tests/test_loc.gd`: a missing key returns the fallback, an empty key returns the fallback, a key added with `var tr := Translation.new(); tr.locale = TranslationServer.get_locale(); tr.add_message(StringName("test.loc.hello"), StringName("Hola")); TranslationServer.add_translation(tr)` returns `"Hola"` (remove it with `TranslationServer.remove_translation(tr)` in `after_each`), `tf("test.loc.none", "{0} of {1}", [2, 5])` gives `"2 of 5"`, and `tf` with a fallback that has no placeholders returns it unchanged (this exact file and class were run on Godot 4.7 and passed). VERIFY: `grep -q 'Loc.t("ui.save_slots.title", "Save Slots")' scripts/mission/save_slot_select.gd && ~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_loc.gd,res://tests/test_save_slot_select.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (Loc does not exist before). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] Route the controls help action labels through Loc - in the row loop of `_populate()` in `scripts/ui/controls_help.gd` pass `Loc.t("controls.action." + action, label)` instead of `label` to `_add_row` (the `ACTIONS` table keeps its English labels as the fallback, so the visible text is unchanged). Create `tests/test_controls_help_loc.gd`: instantiate `res://scenes/controls_help.tscn` as in `tests/test_controls_help.gd` and assert every `ACTIONS` label is still shown; then add a translation for `controls.action.end_turn` (same `Translation` setup and `remove_translation` cleanup as `tests/test_loc.gd`) with the text `"Finish Turn"`, instantiate a fresh view and assert the label texts contain `"Finish Turn"` and not `"End Turn"`. VERIFY: `grep -q 'Loc.t("controls.action." + action, label)' scripts/ui/controls_help.gd && ~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_controls_help_loc.gd,res://tests/test_controls_help.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the translated label is not used before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] Route codex enemy and soldier names through Loc - in `scripts/codex/codex_data.gd` make `enemy_name(t)` return `Loc.t("codex.enemy.%s.name" % enemy_key(t), _ENEMY_NAMES.get(t, "Grunt"))` and `soldier_name(c)` return `Loc.t("codex.soldier.%s.name" % soldier_key(c), <the existing table lookup>)`; with no translation loaded both return exactly what they return today. Create `tests/test_codex_data_loc.gd`: without a translation `enemy_name(Unit.EnemyType.DRONE)` is `"Drone"` and every `CodexData.ENEMY_ORDER` entry still gives its old name; with a translation for `codex.enemy.drone.name` set to `"Dron"` (same setup and cleanup as `tests/test_loc.gd`) it returns `"Dron"` and `display_name_for("enemies", "drone")` also returns `"Dron"`. - [ ] [P3] [decomposed] Add a key extraction tool for the Loc calls - create `tools/extract_loc_keys.py` with `extract_keys(text: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]` returning `(key, english)` pairs for every `Loc.t("key", "english")` and `Loc.tf("key", "english", [...])` call whose first two arguments are plain double-quoted string literals (a backslash-escaped quote inside is unescaped; calls with a non-literal argument are skipped), `collect(root: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]` walking `scripts/**/*.gd` in sorted path order and returning the unique pairs sorted by key, and a `main()` that prints `keys,en` followed by one CSV row per pair using the `csv` module (Godot's translation CSV layout). ### FEATURE: Mission generator tooling and fixes - acceptance: `tools/mission_level_generator.py` can print an ASCII preview and a summary of what it generated, sweep a range of seeds for failures, vary the enemy mix without changing today's default output, set SURVIVE turns, and produces a valid ASSASSINATE mission with a real target; nothing under `missions/` is rewritten.

### FEATURE: Morale text helpers - acceptance: pure static helpers describe a morale value, its panic state and a morale loss as plain text so a unit panel can show it; nothing calls them yet and `scripts/battle/morale.gd` is not edited.
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] Morale text helpers - create `scripts/battle/morale_text.gd` (`class_name MoraleText`, `extends RefCounted`, `const Morale := preload("res://scripts/battle/morale.gd")`) with `static func meter_text(current: int, cap: int) -> String` returning `"Morale %d%%" % Morale.morale_percent(current, cap)`. Tests go in `tests/test_morale_text_meter.gd`: `test_half_morale`, `test_zero_cap_is_zero_percent`, `test_over_cap_is_one_hundred_percent`. `static func status_text(morale: int, threshold: int) -> String` returning `"Steady"` when `Morale.is_panicked(morale, threshold)` is false and `"Panicked, %d below" % Morale.panic_severity(morale, threshold)` otherwise (`status_text(2, 5)` gives `"Panicked, 3 below"`, `status_text(5, 5)` gives `"Steady"`). Tests go in `tests/test_morale_text_status.gd`: `test_at_threshold_is_steady`, `test_two_of_five_is_three_below`, `test_far_above_threshold_is_steady`. `static func loss_text(allies_lost: int, per_loss: int) -> String` returning `"No morale loss"` when `maxi(allies_lost, 0) * maxi(per_loss, 0)` is 0 and `"-%d morale" % loss` otherwise (`loss_text(2, 5)` gives `"-10 morale"`). Tests go in `tests/test_morale_text_loss.gd`: `test_two_lost_at_five_each`, `test_zero_lost_is_no_loss`, `test_negative_per_loss_is_no_loss`. `static func summary(current: int, cap: int, threshold: int) -> String` returning `"%s, %s" % [meter_text(current, cap), status_text(current, threshold)]` (`summary(30, 60, 20)` gives `"Morale 50%, Steady"`, `summary(10, 60, 20)` gives `"Morale 16%, Panicked, 10 below"`). Tests go in `tests/test_morale_text_summary.gd`: `test_steady_summary`, `test_panicked_summary`. Every test file is `extends GutTest` with `const MoraleText := preload("res://scripts/battle/morale_text.gd")`, same shape as `tests/test_star_rating.gd`. VERIFY: `grep -q 'static func meter_text' scripts/battle/morale_text.gd && ~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_morale_text_meter.gd,res://tests/test_morale_text_status.gd,res://tests/test_morale_text_loss.gd,res://tests/test_morale_text_summary.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals. {cat: game; size: S; multifile: yes; research: none}

## Seeded round 23 2026-11-20 (Mark-authorized)
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] Campaign pacing report tool - the campaign is 57 numbered missions plus a tutorial in `missions/mission_*.tres` but nothing shows how enemy count, grid size and objective change mission to mission. Add a read-only, self-contained `tools/campaign_report.py` (standard library only, imports no other tool, edits no mission or script) with own parsers `_vec_list(text, key)` (`re.search(r"(?m)^" + key + r"\s*=\s*Array\[Vector2i\]\(\[(.*?)\]\)", text, re.S)` then `re.findall(r"Vector2i\((-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+)\)", group)`, `[]` when absent) and `_vec(text, key)` (`(?m)^key\s*=\s*Vector2i\((-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+)\)`, `None` when absent). Steps in order: (1) `def mission_row(name: str, text: str) -> dict` returning `{"name", "grid": (w, h), "enemies", "obstacles", "objective", "max_turns", "theme"}` with defaults `(12, 12)`, 0, 8, "wasteland" (as in `scripts/mission/mission_data.gd`; regexes `^objective_type\s*=\s*(\d+)`, `^max_turns\s*=\s*(\d+)`, `terrain_theme\s*=\s*"([^"]*)"`). Create `tools/tests/test_campaign_report.py` (`unittest`; `sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))`, `import campaign_report as cr`, `ROOT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..")`) with `test_mission_05_row` (`missions/mission_05.tres`: grid `(14, 14)`, enemies 6, obstacles 35, objective 0, max_turns 8, theme "farmland") and `test_defaults_for_empty_text`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_campaign_report.py`. Steps in order: (1) `static func canonical(payload: Dictionary) -> String` (`JSON.stringify` with sorted keys, parsed back with `JSON.parse_string` and stringified again with sorted keys, so ints and floats read the same before and after a file round trip) and `static func digest(payload: Dictionary) -> String` (`canonical(payload).sha256_text()`, 64 hex characters). Create `tests/test_save_envelope_digest.gd` (`extends GutTest`, `const SaveEnvelope := preload("res://scripts/save/save_envelope.gd")`) with `test_digest_is_64_chars`, `test_key_order_does_not_matter` (`{"a": 1, "b": 2}` against `{"b": 2, "a": 1}`), `test_value_change_changes_digest`, `test_int_and_whole_float_match` (`{"n": 3}` against `{"n": 3.0}`); VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_save_envelope_digest.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (2) `static func wrap(payload: Dictionary) -> String` (`JSON.stringify({"sha256": digest(payload), "payload": payload}, "", true)`). (3) `static func unwrap(text: String) -> Dictionary` returning `{"ok": true, "payload": <Dictionary>, "error": ""}` for a matching envelope and `{"ok": false, "payload": {}, "error": <message>}` with `"not valid JSON"`, `"not an envelope"` (not a Dictionary, or `sha256` or `payload` missing, or `payload` not a Dictionary) or `"checksum mismatch"`. (4) `static func is_envelope(text: String) -> bool` (true only for valid JSON holding a Dictionary `payload` and a String `sha256`; no checksum comparison). Create `tests/test_save_envelope_is_envelope.gd` with `test_wrapped_text_is_envelope`, `test_plain_save_is_not`, `test_empty_text_is_not`, `test_tampered_text_is_still_an_envelope`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_save_envelope_is_envelope.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

(3) duplicate handling in `push`: a text equal to the newest toast's text refreshes that toast's remaining time instead of adding another; an equal text that is not the newest still adds a toast. ## Seeded round 27 2026-11-25 (Mark-authorized)
- [ ] [P3] [ready] Lethal-first target picker - enemy and ally target choice is spread across `Aggro`, `KillPriority` and inline battle code, and nothing offers one pure function that prefers a kill, then the weakest remainder, then the nearest target. Candidates are dictionaries `{"id", "hp", "damage", "dist"}` (missing keys read as 0). Add pure static helpers in `scripts/battle/target_picker.gd` (`class_name TargetPicker`, `extends RefCounted`); nothing calls them yet and no existing script is edited. Steps in order: (1) `static func lethal_ids(candidates: Array) -> Array` returning the `id` of every candidate whose `damage` is at least its `hp`, in input order, skipping any candidate with `hp` of 0 or less (two candidates `{id 1, hp 4, damage 5}` and `{id 2, hp 6, damage 5}` give `[1]`; an empty array gives `[]`; `{id 3, hp 0, damage 9}` is skipped). Create `tests/test_target_picker_lethal.gd` (`extends GutTest`, `const TargetPicker := preload("res://scripts/battle/target_picker.gd")`) with `test_lethal_listed`, `test_non_lethal_excluded`, `test_dead_target_skipped`, `test_empty_gives_empty`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_target_picker_lethal.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (2) `static func remaining_hp(candidate: Dictionary) -> int` returning `maxi(hp - damage, 0)`, and `static func pick_lowest_remaining(candidates: Array) -> int` returning the `id` with the lowest remaining hp, ties going to the smaller `dist` and then the smaller `id`, `-1` for an empty array (`{id 1, hp 10, damage 4, dist 5}` against `{id 2, hp 8, damage 3, dist 9}` gives `2` because 5 is below 6; equal remainders with dists 3 and 7 pick the dist 3 one; a full tie picks the lower id). Create `tests/test_target_picker_remaining.gd` (same header) with `test_remaining_hp_floors_at_zero`, `test_lowest_remaining_wins`, `test_tie_goes_to_nearer`, `test_full_tie_goes_to_lower_id`, `test_empty_is_minus_one`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_target_picker_remaining.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (3) `static func choose(candidates: Array) -> int` that first looks at `lethal_ids`; when any exist it returns the lethal candidate with the smallest `dist` (ties to the lower id), otherwise it returns `pick_lowest_remaining`; `-1` for an empty array (lethal ids 3 at dist 6 and 5 at dist 2 give `5`; with no lethal candidate the result equals `pick_lowest_remaining`). - [ ] [P3] [ready] Spawn plan helpers - `SpawnBudget.compute(wave, difficulty)` gives one number per wave but nothing sums waves, splits a budget between enemy kinds or trims it to a cap. Add pure static helpers in `scripts/battle/spawn_plan.gd` (`class_name SpawnPlan`, `extends RefCounted`, `const SpawnBudget := preload("res://scripts/battle/spawn_budget.gd")`); nothing calls them yet and no existing script is edited. Steps in order: (1) `static func cumulative(from_wave: int, to_wave: int, difficulty: int) -> int` summing `SpawnBudget.compute(w, difficulty)` for every wave from `from_wave` to `to_wave` inclusive, 0 when `to_wave < from_wave` (difficulty 1: waves 0 to 2 give `5 + 7 + 9 = 21`, wave 1 alone gives `7`, `(2, 1)` gives `0`). Create `tests/test_spawn_plan_cumulative.gd` (`extends GutTest`, `const SpawnPlan := preload("res://scripts/battle/spawn_plan.gd")`, `const SpawnBudget := preload("res://scripts/battle/spawn_budget.gd")`) with `test_sum_over_wave_range`, `test_single_wave`, `test_reversed_range_is_zero`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_spawn_plan_cumulative.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (2) `static func split(total: int, weights: Array) -> Array` dividing `total` by `weights` with the largest remainder method so the parts always add up to `total` (floor each share, then give the leftover units one by one to the largest remainders, ties to the earlier index); negative weights count as 0; `total <= 0` or a weight sum of 0 gives an array of zeros the same size (`split(10, [1, 1, 1])` gives `[4, 3, 3]`, `split(10, [2, 1])` gives `[7, 3]`, `split(5, [0, 0])` gives `[0, 0]`). Create `tests/test_spawn_plan_split.gd` (same header) with `test_even_weights_leftover_goes_first`, `test_two_to_one_split`, `test_parts_always_sum_to_total`, `test_zero_weights_give_zeros`, `test_negative_weight_counts_as_zero`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_spawn_plan_split.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (3) `static func waves_to_reach(target: int, difficulty: int, max_waves: int) -> int` returning the smallest wave `w` from 0 to `max_waves` for which `cumulative(0, w, difficulty)` is at least `target`, `-1` when none does (difficulty 1: target 12 gives `1`, target 21 gives `2`, target 0 gives `0`, target 100 with `max_waves` 3 gives `-1`). Create `tests/test_spawn_plan_reach.gd` (same header) with `test_first_wave_that_reaches_target`, `test_zero_target_is_wave_zero`, `test_unreachable_target_is_minus_one`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_spawn_plan_reach.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func trim_to_cap(counts: Array, cap: int) -> Array` removing units from the last entry backwards until the sum is at most `cap` and no entry goes below 0, returning a copy (`[4, 3, 3]` with cap 8 gives `[4, 3, 1]`, with cap 4 gives `[4, 0, 0]`, with cap 20 is unchanged; the argument is not changed). Create `tests/test_spawn_plan_trim.gd` (same header) with `test_trim_removes_from_the_end`, `test_trim_can_empty_later_entries`, `test_under_cap_is_unchanged`, `test_input_is_not_changed`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_spawn_plan_trim.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

Add pure static helpers in `scripts/battle/reload_plan.gd` (`class_name ReloadPlan`, `extends RefCounted`); nothing calls them yet and no existing script is edited. Steps in order: (1) `static func shots_before_reload(current: int) -> int` returning `maxi(current, 0)` and `static func reloads_needed(shots: int, magazine: int, current: int) -> int` returning how many reloads firing `shots` needs: 0 when `shots <= current`, otherwise `ceil((shots - current) / magazine)`, and `-1` when extra shots are needed but `magazine <= 0` (`reloads_needed(7, 3, 1)` gives `2`, `reloads_needed(1, 3, 1)` gives `0`, `reloads_needed(4, 0, 2)` gives `-1`). Create `tests/test_reload_plan_reloads.gd` (`extends GutTest`, `const ReloadPlan := preload("res://scripts/battle/reload_plan.gd")`) with `test_reloads_across_magazines`, `test_no_reload_when_current_covers_shots`, `test_zero_magazine_is_minus_one`, `test_negative_current_reads_as_zero`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_reload_plan_reloads.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (2) `static func total_ap(shots: int, magazine: int, current: int, shot_cost: int, reload_cost: int) -> int` returning `shots * shot_cost + reloads * reload_cost`, `-1` when `reloads_needed` is `-1` (`total_ap(7, 3, 1, 1, 2)` gives `11`, `total_ap(2, 3, 5, 1, 2)` gives `2`). Create `tests/test_reload_plan_ap.gd` (same header) with `test_total_ap_with_reloads`, `test_total_ap_without_reloads`, `test_impossible_plan_is_minus_one`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_reload_plan_ap.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (3) `static func shots_with_ap(ap: int, magazine: int, current: int, shot_cost: int, reload_cost: int) -> int` simulating shot by shot: a shot costs `shot_cost` when `current > 0`; with an empty magazine a reload (`reload_cost`, refills to `magazine`) is paid first; stop when the next step cannot be paid; return 0 when `shot_cost <= 0`, and stop after 1000 steps (`shots_with_ap(5, 2, 1, 1, 2)` gives `3`, `shots_with_ap(0, 2, 1, 1, 2)` gives `0`, an empty magazine with `magazine` 0 gives `0`). Create `tests/test_reload_plan_withap.gd` (same header) with `test_shots_bought_with_reload_in_between`, `test_zero_ap_buys_no_shots`, `test_empty_magazine_with_zero_size_gives_zero`, `test_zero_shot_cost_gives_zero`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_reload_plan_withap.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func ap_to_empty(current: int, shot_cost: int) -> int` returning `maxi(current, 0) * maxi(shot_cost, 0)` and `static func needs_reload_before(shots: int, current: int) -> bool` that is true when `shots > current` (`ap_to_empty(3, 2)` gives `6`; `needs_reload_before(3, 3)` is false). - [ ] [P3] [ready] Menu focus order helpers - menus rely on engine focus defaults, and tab and arrow order bugs are only found by hand; pure index math for lists and grids of buttons is easy to test. All results are indices, and `-1` means nothing can take focus. Add pure static helpers in `scripts/ui/focus_order.gd` (`class_name FocusOrder`, `extends RefCounted`); nothing calls them yet and no existing script is edited. Steps in order: (1) `static func next_index(count: int, current: int, step: int, wrap: bool) -> int` returning `-1` when `count <= 0`; a `current` below 0 means nothing is focused so `step > 0` gives 0 and `step < 0` gives `count - 1`; otherwise `posmod(current + step, count)` when `wrap` and `clampi(current + step, 0, count - 1)` when not (`(4, 3, 1, true)` gives `0`, `(4, 3, 1, false)` gives `3`, `(4, 0, -1, true)` gives `3`, `(4, -1, 1, true)` gives `0`, `(4, -1, -1, false)` gives `3`, `(0, 0, 1, true)` gives `-1`). Create `tests/test_focus_order_next.gd` (`extends GutTest`, `const FocusOrder := preload("res://scripts/ui/focus_order.gd")`) with `test_wraps_past_the_end`, `test_clamps_at_the_end`, `test_wraps_below_zero`, `test_nothing_focused_forward_gives_first`, `test_nothing_focused_backward_gives_last`, `test_empty_list_gives_minus_one`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_focus_order_next.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (2) `static func grid_neighbor(index: int, cols: int, count: int, dx: int, dy: int) -> int` for buttons laid out row by row in `cols` columns: the new index after moving by `dx` columns and `dy` rows, or `index` unchanged when the move would leave the grid, cross a row edge or land at or past `count` (`cols` 3, `count` 8: index 4 with `dy` 1 gives `7`, index 7 with `dy` 1 gives `7`, index 2 with `dx` 1 gives `2`, index 0 with `dx` -1 gives `0`; `cols` of 0 or less returns `index`). Create `tests/test_focus_order_grid.gd` (same header) with `test_move_down_one_row`, `test_move_down_past_last_row_stays`, `test_move_right_at_row_edge_stays`, `test_move_left_at_first_column_stays`, `test_zero_columns_returns_index`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_focus_order_grid.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (3) `static func first_enabled(enabled: Array) -> int` returning the first index whose entry is true, `-1` when none, and `static func next_enabled(enabled: Array, current: int, step: int) -> int` stepping by `step` (1 or -1) with wrap, skipping disabled entries, `-1` when none is enabled (`[false, true, false, true]`: from 1 forward gives `3`, from 3 forward gives `1`, from 1 backward gives `3`). Create `tests/test_focus_order_enabled.gd` (same header) with `test_first_enabled_found`, `test_first_enabled_none_is_minus_one`, `test_next_enabled_skips_disabled`, `test_next_enabled_wraps`, `test_next_enabled_backward`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_focus_order_enabled.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func tab_order(positions: Array) -> Array` returning indices of `Vector2` positions sorted by y then x, stable for equal positions (`[(10, 5), (0, 5), (3, 0)]` gives `[2, 1, 0]`; an empty array gives `[]`). Create `tests/test_focus_order_tab.gd` (same header) with `test_rows_sort_before_columns`, `test_equal_positions_keep_input_order`, `test_empty_gives_empty`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_focus_order_tab.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

Font sizes are integers in pixels; the large text percent is passed in by the caller. Add pure static helpers in `scripts/ui/text_scale.gd` (`class_name TextScale`, `extends RefCounted`); nothing calls them yet and no existing script is edited. (2) `static func with_large_text(base: int, ui_pct: int, large_pct: int, large_on: bool) -> int` applying `large_pct` first when `large_on` and `large_pct > 0`, then `scaled_size` (`(16, 100, 125, true)` gives `20`, `(16, 100, 125, false)` gives `16`, `(16, 100, 0, true)` gives `16`). (3) `static func line_height(font_size: int, spacing_pct: int) -> int` returning `roundi(font_size * spacing_pct / 100.0)` but never less than `font_size` (`(16, 125)` gives `20`, `(16, 50)` gives `16`, `(16, 100)` gives `16`). Create `tests/test_text_scale_line.gd` (same header) with `test_line_height_with_spacing`, `test_line_height_never_below_font_size`, `test_hundred_percent_equals_font_size`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_text_scale_line.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func fits(text: String, font_size: int, avg_char_width_pct: int, max_width_px: int) -> bool` using integer maths `text.length() * font_size * avg_char_width_pct <= max_width_px * 100` (`("abcd", 10, 60, 24)` is true, `("abcd", 10, 60, 23)` is false, an empty string is true). (3) `static func wrap_words(text: String, width: int) -> Array` returning lines built greedily from space separated words so no line passes `width` unless one word is longer, which then gets its own line; `[text]` when `width <= 0`, `[]` for blank text (`("one two three", 7)` gives `["one two", "three"]`, `("abcdefghij k", 5)` gives `["abcdefghij", "k"]`). Steps in order: (1) `static func after_percent(damage: int, percent: int) -> int` returning `damage * (100 - clampi(percent, 0, 100)) / 100` rounded down, 0 for negative damage (`(10, 50)` gives `5`, `(10, 150)` gives `0`, `(10, -20)` gives `10`, `(-4, 50)` gives `0`). Distances are pixels and times are milliseconds. Add pure static helpers in `scripts/input/gesture_math.gd` (`class_name GestureMath`, `extends RefCounted`); nothing calls them yet and no existing script is edited. Steps in order: (1) `static func swipe_direction(delta: Vector2, min_px: float) -> String` returning `"none"` when the longer axis of `delta` is below `min_px`, otherwise `"right"`, `"left"`, `"down"` or `"up"` by the dominant axis, a tie going to the horizontal axis (`((30, 5), 20)` gives `"right"`, `((-4, -40), 20)` gives `"up"`, `((10, 10), 5)` gives `"right"`, `((3, 3), 20)` gives `"none"`). (2) `static func pinch_ratio(start_distance: float, now_distance: float) -> float` returning `now_distance / start_distance`, `1.0` when `start_distance <= 0.0` (`(100.0, 150.0)` gives `1.5`, `(100.0, 50.0)` gives `0.5`, `(0.0, 80.0)` gives `1.0`; use `assert_almost_eq`). Create `tests/test_gesture_math_pinch.gd` (same header) with `test_spread_gives_ratio_above_one`, `test_squeeze_gives_ratio_below_one`, `test_zero_start_gives_one`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_gesture_math_pinch.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (3) `static func is_tap(down_ms: int, move_px: float, max_ms: int, max_move: float) -> bool` that is true when `down_ms <= max_ms` and `move_px <= max_move` (`(120, 4.0, 200, 10.0)` is true, `(250, 4.0, 200, 10.0)` is false, `(120, 12.0, 200, 10.0)` is false, exact limits are true). Create `tests/test_gesture_math_tap.gd` (same header) with `test_quick_still_press_is_a_tap`, `test_long_press_is_not_a_tap`, `test_dragged_press_is_not_a_tap`, `test_exact_limits_are_a_tap`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_gesture_math_tap.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func is_double_tap(prev_ms: int, now_ms: int, prev_pos: Vector2, now_pos: Vector2, window_ms: int, radius: float) -> bool` that is true when `prev_ms >= 0`, `now_ms - prev_ms` is between 0 and `window_ms` and the positions are within `radius` (`(1000, 1250, (10, 10), (12, 11), 300, 20.0)` is true; a first tap time of -1 is false; 400 ms apart is false; 50 px apart is false). - [ ] [P3] [ready] Camera bounds helpers - the battle camera pans and zooms by touch and nothing keeps it inside the map; the clamp and centring maths are pure. The camera offset is the top-left corner of the view in map pixels, `margin` is extra scroll allowed past each edge. Add pure static helpers in `scripts/ui/camera_bounds.gd` (`class_name CameraBounds`, `extends RefCounted`); nothing calls them yet and no existing script is edited. Steps in order: (1) `static func clamp_offset(offset: Vector2, map_px: Vector2, view_px: Vector2, margin: float) -> Vector2` clamping each axis into `[-margin, map + margin - view]`, and centring on that axis (`(map - view) / 2`) when the map plus margins is smaller than the view (map `(1000, 800)`, view `(400, 300)`, margin 0, offset `(-50, 600)` gives `(0, 500)`; map width 200 with view width 400 gives x `-100`). Create `tests/test_camera_bounds_clamp.gd` (`extends GutTest`, `const CameraBounds := preload("res://scripts/ui/camera_bounds.gd")`) with `test_offset_inside_bounds_is_unchanged`, `test_offset_clamped_to_both_edges`, `test_small_map_is_centred`, `test_margin_allows_overscroll`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_camera_bounds_clamp.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (2) `static func center_on(point: Vector2, view_px: Vector2) -> Vector2` returning `point - view_px / 2.0` and `static func center_clamped(point: Vector2, map_px: Vector2, view_px: Vector2, margin: float) -> Vector2` that applies `clamp_offset` to it (`center_on((500, 400), (400, 300))` gives `(300, 250)`; centring on `(0, 0)` with margin 0 clamps to `(0, 0)`). Create `tests/test_camera_bounds_center.gd` (same header) with `test_center_on_point`, `test_centering_near_corner_is_clamped`, `test_centering_in_middle_is_unchanged`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_camera_bounds_center.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (3) `static func zoom_clamp(zoom: float, min_zoom: float, max_zoom: float) -> float` that clamps into the range, swapping the limits when `min_zoom > max_zoom` (`(3.0, 0.5, 2.0)` gives `2.0`, `(0.1, 0.5, 2.0)` gives `0.5`, `(1.0, 2.0, 0.5)` gives `1.0`). Create `tests/test_camera_bounds_zoomclamp.gd` (same header) with `test_zoom_clamps_high`, `test_zoom_clamps_low`, `test_reversed_limits_are_swapped`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_camera_bounds_zoomclamp.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func zoom_step(zoom: float, steps: int, factor: float, min_zoom: float, max_zoom: float) -> float` returning `zoom_clamp(zoom * pow(factor, steps), min_zoom, max_zoom)` (`(1.0, 2, 1.5, 0.5, 2.0)` gives `2.0`, `(1.0, -1, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0)` gives `0.5`, zero steps leaves zoom unchanged; use `assert_almost_eq`). Create `tests/test_camera_bounds_zoomstep.gd` (same header) with `test_zoom_in_steps`, `test_zoom_out_step`, `test_zero_steps_is_unchanged`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_camera_bounds_zoomstep.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

- [ ] [P3] [ready] Seeded roll helpers - replays and bug reports need rolls that can be reproduced from a number, and gameplay code uses the engine random generator directly. A small pure generator keeps the maths testable. All values stay below 2147483648 so they are exact in 64 bit integers. Add pure static helpers in `scripts/battle/seeded_roll.gd` (`class_name SeededRoll`, `extends RefCounted`); nothing calls them yet and no existing script is edited. Steps in order: (1) `static func next_state(state: int) -> int` returning `(state * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff` (`next_state(0)` gives `12345`, `next_state(1)` gives `1103527590`; the result is never negative and below 2147483648 for states 0, 1, 12345 and 2147483647). Create `tests/test_seeded_roll_state.gd` (`extends GutTest`, `const SeededRoll := preload("res://scripts/battle/seeded_roll.gd")`) with `test_next_state_from_zero`, `test_next_state_from_one`, `test_next_state_stays_in_range`, `test_same_state_gives_same_result`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_seeded_roll_state.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (2) `static func roll(state: int, sides: int) -> int` returning `(state >> 8) % sides + 1`, `0` when `sides <= 0` (`roll(0, 6)` gives `1`, `roll(12345, 6)` gives `1` because 12345 shifted is 48, `roll(12345, 100)` gives `49`, `roll(5, 0)` gives `0`). Create `tests/test_seeded_roll_roll.gd` (same header) with `test_roll_from_zero_state`, `test_roll_uses_shifted_state`, `test_roll_with_hundred_sides`, `test_zero_sides_gives_zero`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_seeded_roll_roll.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (3) `static func percent_hit(state: int, percent: int) -> bool` that is true when `roll(state, 100) <= percent`, always false for `percent <= 0` and always true for `percent >= 100` (`percent_hit(12345, 49)` is true, `percent_hit(12345, 48)` is false). Create `tests/test_seeded_roll_hit.gd` (same header) with `test_hit_at_exact_roll`, `test_miss_below_roll`, `test_zero_percent_never_hits`, `test_hundred_percent_always_hits`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_seeded_roll_hit.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func sequence(seed_value: int, count: int, sides: int) -> Array` that advances the state with `next_state` before each roll and returns the rolls, `[]` when `count <= 0` (the first value equals `roll(next_state(seed_value), sides)`; two calls with the same arguments return equal arrays; every value is between 1 and `sides`; the length is `count`). Create `tests/test_seeded_roll_sequence.gd` (same header) with `test_first_value_follows_next_state`, `test_same_seed_gives_same_sequence`, `test_values_stay_within_sides`, `test_length_matches_count`, `test_zero_count_gives_empty`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_seeded_roll_sequence.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

(2) `static func expected_with_crit(hit_percent: int, crit_percent: int, damage: int, crit_damage: int) -> int` returning `hit * ((100 - crit) * damage + crit * crit_damage) / 10000` with both percents clamped to 0..100 (`(80, 25, 10, 20)` gives `10`, `(100, 0, 7, 99)` gives `7`, `(100, 100, 7, 9)` gives `9`). Create `tests/test_expected_damage_crit.gd` (same header) with `test_expected_with_crit`, `test_zero_crit_ignores_crit_damage`, `test_certain_crit_uses_crit_damage`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_expected_damage_crit.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (3) `static func best_index(options: Array) -> int` over `{"hit": int, "damage": int}` options returning the index of the highest `expected`, ties to the lower index, `-1` for an empty array (`[{hit 50, damage 10}, {hit 90, damage 7}]` gives `1` because 5 is below 6; `[{hit 50, damage 10}, {hit 100, damage 5}]` is a tie on 5 and gives `0`). Create `tests/test_expected_damage_best.gd` (same header) with `test_best_option_found`, `test_tie_goes_to_lower_index`, `test_empty_options_give_minus_one`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_expected_damage_best.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func shots_to_kill(hp: int, hit_percent: int, damage: int) -> int` returning `ceil(hp / expected)` using `expected(hit_percent, damage)`, `-1` when that is 0, and `0` when `hp <= 0` (`(10, 50, 4)` gives `5`, `(10, 0, 4)` gives `-1`, `(0, 50, 4)` gives `0`). Create `tests/test_expected_damage_kill.gd` (same header) with `test_shots_to_kill_rounds_up`, `test_zero_expected_gives_minus_one`, `test_zero_hp_needs_no_shots`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_expected_damage_kill.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

Add pure static helpers in `scripts/mission/par_estimate.gd` (`class_name ParEstimate`, `extends RefCounted`); nothing calls them yet and no existing script is edited. Steps in order: (1) `static func turns_to_cross(distance: int, move_range: int) -> int` returning `ceil(distance / move_range)`, `0` when `distance <= 0` and `-1` when `move_range <= 0` (`(10, 4)` gives `3`, `(8, 4)` gives `2`, `(0, 4)` gives `0`, `(5, 0)` gives `-1`). Create `tests/test_par_estimate_cross.gd` (`extends GutTest`, `const ParEstimate := preload("res://scripts/mission/par_estimate.gd")`) with `test_distance_rounds_up_to_whole_turns`, `test_exact_multiple`, `test_zero_distance_is_zero`, `test_zero_move_range_is_minus_one`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_par_estimate_cross.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (2) `static func chebyshev_distances(from: Vector2i, targets: Array) -> Array` returning the Chebyshev distance to each target in order (`((0, 0), [(3, 1), (2, 5)])` gives `[3, 5]`; no targets gives `[]`). Create `tests/test_par_estimate_distances.gd` (same header) with `test_distances_in_order`, `test_no_targets_gives_empty`, `test_target_on_start_is_zero`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_par_estimate_distances.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (3) `static func par_turns(distances: Array, move_range: int, buffer: int) -> int` returning the largest `turns_to_cross` over the distances plus `maxi(buffer, 0)`, `maxi(buffer, 0)` for no distances, `-1` when `move_range <= 0` (`([10, 4], 4, 2)` gives `5`, `([], 4, 2)` gives `2`, `([4], 0, 2)` gives `-1`). Create `tests/test_par_estimate_par.gd` (same header) with `test_par_uses_farthest_target`, `test_no_targets_returns_buffer`, `test_zero_move_range_is_minus_one`, `test_negative_buffer_reads_as_zero`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_par_estimate_par.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func par_for_mission(player_cell: Vector2i, enemy_cells: Array, move_range: int, buffer: int) -> int` composing `chebyshev_distances` and `par_turns` (player `(0, 0)`, enemies `[(10, 0), (4, 4)]`, range 4, buffer 2 gives `5`). Create `tests/test_par_estimate_mission.gd` (same header) with `test_par_for_mission`, `test_no_enemies_gives_buffer`, `test_closer_enemies_lower_par`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_par_estimate_mission.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

Steps in order: (1) `static func keep_target(previous_id: int, valid_ids: Array) -> bool` that is true when `previous_id` is in `valid_ids` and not negative (`(3, [1, 3])` is true, `(3, [1])` false, `(-1, [-1])` false). Create `tests/test_target_lock_keep.gd` (`extends GutTest`, `const TargetLock := preload("res://scripts/battle/target_lock.gd")`) with `test_previous_target_kept_when_valid`, `test_invalid_previous_is_dropped`, `test_negative_id_is_never_kept`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_target_lock_keep.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (2) `static func choose(previous_id: int, valid_ids: Array, fresh_pick: int) -> int` returning `previous_id` when `keep_target` is true, otherwise `fresh_pick` (`(3, [1, 3], 1)` gives `3`, `(3, [1], 1)` gives `1`). (3) `static func lock_turns_left(locked_turn: int, now_turn: int, max_lock: int) -> int` returning `max_lock - (now_turn - locked_turn)` floored at 0, and 0 when `now_turn < locked_turn` (`(2, 3, 3)` gives `2`, `(2, 9, 3)` gives `0`, `(5, 3, 3)` gives `0`). Create `tests/test_target_lock_turns.gd` (same header) with `test_lock_counts_down`, `test_expired_lock_is_zero`, `test_clock_going_backwards_is_zero`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_target_lock_turns.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func should_switch(current_score: int, challenger_score: int, margin: int) -> bool` that is true when `challenger_score >= current_score + margin` (`(10, 15, 5)` is true, `(10, 14, 5)` is false, margin 0 with equal scores is true). Steps in order: (1) `static func remember(memory: Dictionary, id: int, cell: Vector2i, turn: int) -> Dictionary` returning a copy with `id` set to `{"cell": cell, "turn": turn}`; the argument is not changed (remembering id 4 at `(2, 3)` on turn 5 adds that entry; a second call for id 4 replaces it). Create `tests/test_vision_memory_remember.gd` (`extends GutTest`, `const VisionMemory := preload("res://scripts/battle/vision_memory.gd")`) with `test_remember_adds_entry`, `test_remember_replaces_older_entry`, `test_remember_does_not_change_input`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_vision_memory_remember.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (2) `static func last_known(memory: Dictionary, id: int) -> Vector2i` returning the stored cell or `Vector2i(-1, -1)` and `static func age(memory: Dictionary, id: int, turn: int) -> int` returning `maxi(turn - stored_turn, 0)`, `-1` for an unknown id (stored on turn 5: age at turn 8 is `3`, age at turn 4 is `0`, unknown id is `-1`). Create `tests/test_vision_memory_lookup.gd` (same header) with `test_last_known_cell`, `test_unknown_id_gives_sentinel_cell`, `test_age_counts_turns`, `test_age_never_negative`, `test_unknown_id_age_is_minus_one`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_vision_memory_lookup.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (3) `static func forget_old(memory: Dictionary, turn: int, max_age: int) -> Dictionary` returning a copy without entries whose `age` is above `max_age` (entries from turns 1 and 5 at turn 6 with `max_age` 2 keep only the turn 5 entry; `max_age` 5 keeps both). Create `tests/test_vision_memory_forget.gd` (same header) with `test_old_entries_dropped`, `test_entries_at_max_age_kept`, `test_forget_does_not_change_input`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_vision_memory_forget.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). (4) `static func freshest_id(memory: Dictionary) -> int` returning the id with the greatest stored turn, ties to the lower id, `-1` for an empty memory, and `static func ids_sorted(memory: Dictionary) -> Array` returning the ids ascending. Create `tests/test_vision_memory_freshest.gd` (same header) with `test_freshest_id_found`, `test_tie_goes_to_lower_id`, `test_empty_memory_gives_minus_one`, `test_ids_sorted_ascending`; VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_vision_memory_freshest.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw output shows the dashed `All tests passed` success line (no failing or risky tests). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

Create `tools/tests/test_mission_force_ratio.py` (`unittest`; `sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))`, `import mission_force_ratio as m`, `ROOT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..")`) as the tests grow. Steps in order: (1) `parse_counts(text: str) -> dict` returning `{"players": n, "enemies": n}` from the number of `Vector2i(` entries in `player_spawns` and `enemy_spawns` of a mission `.tres` text (a sample with 2 and 3 entries gives `{"players": 2, "enemies": 3}`; absent lists count 0). Tests: `test_counts_from_sample`, `test_missing_lists_are_zero`, `test_other_vector_fields_are_not_counted`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_mission_force_ratio.py`. (2) `ratio(players: int, enemies: int) -> float` returning `enemies / players` and `0.0` when `players` is 0, and `classify(value: float, low: float, high: float) -> str` returning `"low"` below `low`, `"high"` above `high`, else `"ok"` (`ratio(4, 6)` is `1.5`; `classify(0.4, 0.5, 4.0)` is `"low"`; exactly `0.5` and exactly `4.0` are `"ok"`). Tests: `test_ratio_value`, `test_zero_players_gives_zero`, `test_classify_low`, `test_classify_high`, `test_limits_are_inclusive`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_mission_force_ratio.py`. (3) `scan(root: str) -> list` returning sorted `(name, players, enemies, ratio)` for each `missions/mission_*.tres` under `root`, skipping other file names (use `tempfile.TemporaryDirectory` with one valid and one `ManuallyCreated1.tres` file). Tests: `test_scan_lists_mission_files`, `test_scan_skips_other_names`, `test_scan_is_sorted_by_name`, `test_missing_missions_folder_gives_empty`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_mission_force_ratio.py`. (4) `main(argv: list) -> int` (`--root` default the repo root, `--low` default 0.5, `--high` default 4.0, `--strict`) printing `<name> <players> <enemies> <ratio:.2f> <class>` lines and a last line `missions: N, low: a, high: b`, returning 1 only with `--strict` and a or b above 0. Tests: `test_report_mode_returns_zero`, `test_strict_with_high_ratio_returns_one`, `test_strict_with_ok_ratios_returns_zero`, `test_repo_root_run_returns_zero`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_mission_force_ratio.py`. {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

(4) `main(argv: list) -> int` (`--root`, `--min` default 4, `--strict`) printing `<name> min <a> centroid <b>` lines and a last line `close starts: N` counting missions whose `min_gap` is between 0 and `--min` minus 1, returning 1 only with `--strict` and N above 0. - [ ] [P3] [ready] PNG dimension audit tool - sprites in one folder are meant to share a frame size but nothing checks it, and a wrong size shows up as a shifted or squashed unit. A PNG stores its width and height as two big endian 32 bit integers at byte offsets 16 and 20. Add a read-only, self-contained `tools/png_dimension_audit.py` (standard library only, imports no other tool, edits no repo file). Create `tools/tests/test_png_dimension_audit.py` (`unittest`; `sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))`, `import png_dimension_audit as m`, `ROOT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..")`) as the tests grow. Steps in order: (1) `png_size(data: bytes) -> tuple` returning `(width, height)` read with `struct.unpack(">II", data[16:24])` after checking the 8 byte signature `b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"`, and `None` for anything shorter than 24 bytes or with another signature (a header built in the test for 64 by 32 gives `(64, 32)`). Tests: `test_size_read_from_header`, `test_short_data_gives_none`, `test_wrong_signature_gives_none`, `test_non_square_size`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_png_dimension_audit.py`. (2) `scan(root: str) -> list` returning sorted `(rel_path, width, height)` for every `.png` under `<root>/assets` that `png_size` can read, with forward slashes (a temp tree with one valid PNG header and one text file named `x.png` gives one entry). Tests: `test_scan_lists_valid_pngs`, `test_unreadable_png_is_skipped`, `test_scan_is_sorted_by_path`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_png_dimension_audit.py`. (3) `by_folder(entries: list) -> dict` mapping each folder to the sorted list of distinct `(width, height)` pairs found in it, and `mixed_folders(groups: dict) -> list` returning the sorted folders that hold more than one size (two sizes in `assets/sprites/units` are mixed; one size is not). Tests: `test_sizes_grouped_by_folder`, `test_mixed_folders_listed`, `test_single_size_folder_is_not_mixed`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_png_dimension_audit.py`. (4) `main(argv: list) -> int` (`--root`, `--strict`) printing `<folder>: <WxH list>` lines and a last line `mixed folders: N`, returning 1 only with `--strict` and N above 0. Tests: `test_report_mode_returns_zero`, `test_strict_with_mixed_folder_returns_one`, `test_strict_with_uniform_folders_returns_zero`, `test_repo_root_run_returns_zero`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_png_dimension_audit.py`. {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

- [ ] [P3] [ready] GDScript duplicate function check tool - GDScript refuses a script that declares the same function name twice and the failure only shows when Godot loads it; a line by line merge by the fleet can leave a duplicate that no unit test notices. Add a read-only, self-contained `tools/gd_duplicate_funcs.py` (standard library only, imports no other tool, edits no repo file). Create `tools/tests/test_gd_duplicate_funcs.py` (`unittest`; `sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))`, `import gd_duplicate_funcs as m`, `ROOT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..")`) as the tests grow. Steps in order: (1) `func_names(text: str) -> list` returning `(line_number, name)` for each line matching `(?m)^(?:static\s+)?func\s+(\w+)` in file order (1-based lines; an indented nested `func` inside a class body is matched only when it starts the line). Tests: `test_function_names_found`, `test_static_function_found`, `test_commented_function_is_ignored`, `test_line_numbers_are_one_based`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_gd_duplicate_funcs.py`. (2) `duplicates(entries: list) -> list` returning `(name, [line numbers])` for names that appear more than once, sorted by first line (names `a` at 3 and 9 give `("a", [3, 9])`). Tests: `test_duplicate_name_listed_with_lines`, `test_unique_names_give_empty`, `test_three_copies_listed_once`, `test_sorted_by_first_line`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_gd_duplicate_funcs.py`. (3) `scan(root: str) -> list` returning sorted `(rel_path, name, lines)` over `*.gd` under `scripts` and `tests` (the test folder counts because a duplicate `test_` function is also a parse error), skipping files that declare an `inner class` marker line `class ` before the duplicate (a conservative skip so nested classes do not cause false positives). Tests: `test_scan_reports_duplicate_in_scripts`, `test_scan_reports_duplicate_in_tests`, `test_scan_skips_files_with_inner_classes`, `test_scan_is_sorted`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_gd_duplicate_funcs.py`. (4) `main(argv: list) -> int` (`--root`, `--strict`) printing `<path>: <name> lines <a, b>` and a last line `duplicates: N`, returning 1 only with `--strict` and N above 0. Tests: `test_report_mode_returns_zero`, `test_strict_with_duplicate_returns_one`, `test_strict_with_clean_tree_returns_zero`, `test_repo_root_run_returns_zero`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_gd_duplicate_funcs.py`. {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}

Steps in order: (1) `is_junk(path: str) -> bool` that is true for paths containing `__pycache__/`, ending in `.pyc`, `.DS_Store`, `.swp` or `.tmp`, ending in `.import` or starting with `.godot/` (`"tools/x/__pycache__/a.pyc"` true, `"scripts/a.gd"` false, `"icon.svg.import"` true). (2) `junk_in(paths: list) -> list` returning the sorted junk paths and `by_reason(paths: list) -> dict` counting them under the keys `pycache`, `os`, `editor`, `import` (all four keys always present; `.DS_Store` is `os`, `.swp` and `.tmp` are `editor`, `__pycache__` and `.pyc` are `pycache`, `.import` and `.godot/` are `import`). Tests: `test_junk_in_is_sorted`, `test_by_reason_has_all_four_keys`, `test_by_reason_counts`, `test_empty_list_gives_zero_counts`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_tracked_junk_check.py`. (3) `tracked_files(root: str) -> list` running `subprocess.run(["git", "ls-files"], cwd=root, capture_output=True, text=True)` and returning the non-empty lines, an empty list when git fails or the folder is not a repository (a `tempfile.TemporaryDirectory` that is not a git repo gives `[]`). Tests: `test_non_repository_gives_empty_list`, `test_repository_lists_tracked_file`, `test_untracked_file_is_not_listed`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_tracked_junk_check.py`. (4) `main(argv: list) -> int` (`--root`, `--strict`) printing `junk: <path>` lines and a last line `tracked junk: N (pycache a, os b, editor c, import d)`, returning 1 only with `--strict` and N above 0. - [ ] [P3] [ready] GDScript file name convention check tool - Godot expects `class_name RosterManager` to live in `roster_manager.gd` by convention and a mismatch makes scripts hard to find; a few modules (for example `LosClear` in `los_clear.gd`) follow it and others may not, and nothing checks. Add a read-only, self-contained `tools/class_filename_check.py` (standard library only, imports no other tool, edits no repo file). Create `tools/tests/test_class_filename_check.py` (`unittest`; `sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))`, `import class_filename_check as m`, `ROOT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..")`) as the tests grow. Steps in order: (1) `snake_case(name: str) -> str` converting a PascalCase class name to snake case by inserting an underscore before each capital that follows a lower case letter or digit and lower casing (`"RosterManager"` gives `"roster_manager"`, `"Aoe"` gives `"aoe"`, `"UiScale"` gives `"ui_scale"`, `"A"` gives `"a"`, `""` gives `""`). Tests: `test_two_words`, `test_single_word`, `test_short_prefix_word`, `test_single_letter`, `test_empty_name`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_class_filename_check.py`. (2) `class_name_of(text: str) -> str` returning the first `(?m)^class_name\s+(\w+)` match or `""`, and `mismatch(rel_path: str, text: str) -> str` returning `""` when the file has no `class_name` or `snake_case(class)` equals the file stem, otherwise `"<class> should be in <expected>.gd"`. Tests: `test_class_name_read`, `test_no_class_name_is_empty`, `test_matching_file_has_no_mismatch`, `test_mismatch_message`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_class_filename_check.py`. (3) `scan(root: str) -> list` returning sorted `(rel_path, message)` for `*.gd` under `<root>/scripts` skipping `rig_test` and `tools` folders, where `message` is non-empty. Tests: `test_scan_reports_mismatch`, `test_scan_ignores_matching_files`, `test_scan_skips_rig_test_folder`, `test_scan_is_sorted`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_class_filename_check.py`. (4) `main(argv: list) -> int` (`--root`, `--strict`) printing `<path>: <message>` lines and a last line `name mismatches: N`, returning 1 only with `--strict` and N above 0. Tests: `test_report_mode_returns_zero`, `test_strict_with_mismatch_returns_one`, `test_strict_with_clean_tree_returns_zero`, `test_repo_root_run_returns_zero`; VERIFY: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/tests -p test_class_filename_check.py`. {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}
